         if h == 'Integer':
             if len(a) != 1 or a[0].head != 'Number':
                 raise InputError('invalid Integer')

ASSEMBLY SCRIPT (mechanical report reproducibility)

FILE _scratch/s11c/o2-comparator-build-r3/collect_measurements.py
"""Mechanical report assembly from completed run artifacts; no CAS imports."""
from collections import Counter
import hashlib
import json
from pathlib import Path
import re
import shlex
import subprocess

root=Path('/var/projects/toy_physics')
scratch=root/'_scratch/s11c/o2-comparator-build-r3'
measurement=root/'research/pde_ledger_v3/_measurements/O2_comparator_builder_r3_measurements.txt'
for run in ('tests-01','tests-02','ablations-01','full-01'):
    receipt=json.loads((scratch/run/'outcome.json').read_text())
    if receipt['exitCode'] or not receipt['limitsVerified'] or receipt['childOutcome']['guardReason']:
        raise RuntimeError('incomplete or operationally unsuccessful run: '+run)

catalog=json.loads((scratch/'catalog.json').read_text())
accounting=[json.loads(line) for line in (scratch/'accounting.jsonl').read_text().splitlines()]
summary={'tables':{key:len(catalog[key]) for key in ('joins','names','actions','balances')},
         'accounting':dict(Counter(r['accounting'] for r in accounting if 'accounting' in r)),
         'stream_census':{},'output_kinds':{},'comparison_outcomes':{},'ablation_copies':{}}
for engine in ('py','wl'):
    rows=[r for r in accounting if r.get('kind')=='stream_object_count' and r['engine']==engine]
    summary['stream_census'][engine]={'objects':len(rows),**{
        field:sum(r[field] for r in rows) for field in
        ('parsed_leaves','accounted_leaves','compared_leaves','unaccounted_leaves')}}
summary['generic_unjoined_reasons']=sum(r.get('reason','').startswith('No declared object counterpart') for r in accounting)
kinds,outcomes=Counter(),Counter()
verdict_tokens=Counter()
def inventory(result):
    outcomes[result['outcome']]+=1
    for child in result.get('children',{}).values():
        inventory(child)
    for child in result.get('operands',[]):
        inventory(child)
for line in (scratch/'comparison.jsonl').open():
    row=json.loads(line)
    kinds[row['kind']]+=1
    if row['kind']=='residual':
        inventory(row['comparison'])
    verdict_tokens.update(re.findall(r'\b(?:PASS|FAIL|AGREE|VERDICT|STATUS)\b',line))
summary['output_kinds']=dict(kinds)
summary['comparison_outcomes']=dict(outcomes)
summary['literal_verdict_tokens_in_comparator_output']=dict(verdict_tokens)
ablations=json.loads((scratch/'ablation-copies/summary.json').read_text())
for row in ablations:
    if row['exit_code']!=1 or row['errors'] or not any(row['required_test'] in v for v in row['failing_tests']):
        raise RuntimeError('ablation evidence incomplete: '+row['ablation'])
    summary['ablation_copies'][row['ablation']]={
        'assertion_failures':len(row['failing_tests']),
        'test_methods':sorted(set(v.split()[0] for v in row['failing_tests'])),
        'required_test':row['required_test'],'resources':row['resources']}
summary['ablation_max_peak_rss_kib']=max(int(re.search(r'peak_rss_kib=(\d+)',row['resources'])[1]) for row in ablations)
summary['test_resources']=(scratch/'tests-02-resources.txt').read_text().strip()
summary['full_resources']=json.loads((scratch/'full-01/stdout').read_text())
(scratch/'validation.json').write_text(json.dumps(summary,indent=2)+'\n')

with measurement.open('w') as out:
    def write(value):
        out.write(value+'\n')
    def literal(path):
        write('\nFILE '+str(path.relative_to(root)))
        write(path.read_text())
    write('O2 comparator builder r3 — commands and literal outputs; no residual target supplied.')
    write('BASELINE '+subprocess.check_output(['git','rev-parse','HEAD'],cwd=root,text=True).strip())
    write('Assembly command: python _scratch/s11c/o2-comparator-build-r3/collect_measurements.py')
    write('DELIVERABLE: canonical OPEN object deltas, result-path controls, guarded ablations and comparison, builder report. No commit or spawned agent.')
    write('\nCOMMAND datalad get research/pde_ledger_v3/scripts/out/O2_live_balance_sympy_audit.out research/pde_ledger_v3/mathematica/out/O2_live_balance_mathematica_audit.out')
    literal(scratch/'datalad-get.log')
    for run in ('tests-01','tests-02','ablations-01','full-01'):
        spec=json.loads((scratch/run/'invocation.json').read_text())
        command=['python','scripts/s11c_guarded_run.py','--pool','o2-comparator','--memory-gib','6',
                 '--log-directory',str((scratch/run).relative_to(root)),'--',*spec['command']]
        write('\nCOMMAND '+shlex.join(command))
        for file in ('stdout','stderr','outcome.json','effective-limits.json','duration-validation.json'):
            literal(scratch/run/file)
    literal(scratch/'tests-02-resources.txt')
    for row in ablations:
        write('\nABLATION '+row['ablation']+' COMMAND '+shlex.join(row['command']))
        for file in ('change.diff','stdout','stderr','resources.txt'):
            literal(scratch/'ablation-copies'/row['ablation']/file)
    write('\nMECHANICAL INVENTORY (counts and stored fields only; no residual interpretation)')
    literal(scratch/'validation.json')
    literal(scratch/'source-preservation.json')
    write('\nDECLARED TABLES (complete rows, both construction-line citations and spec objects)')
    literal(scratch/'catalog.json')
    write('\nPER-ROW ACCOUNTING (complete parsed/compared counts and every unjoined reason)')
    literal(scratch/'accounting.jsonl')
    write('\nSHA256 AND BYTES (streamed output remains in the new scratch directory)')
    paths=[root/'research/pde_ledger_v3/scripts/O2_cross_engine_comparator.py',
           root/'research/pde_ledger_v3/scripts/test_O2_cross_engine_comparator.py',
           root/'research/pde_ledger_v3/scripts/ablate_O2_cross_engine_comparator.py',
           root/'research/pde_ledger_v3/scripts/out/O2_live_balance_sympy_audit.out',
           root/'research/pde_ledger_v3/mathematica/out/O2_live_balance_mathematica_audit.out',
           scratch/'comparison.jsonl',scratch/'catalog.json',scratch/'accounting.jsonl']
    for path in paths:
        digest=hashlib.sha256()
        with path.open('rb') as stream:
            for chunk in iter(lambda:stream.read(1024*1024),b''):
                digest.update(chunk)
        write(json.dumps({'path':str(path.relative_to(root)),'sha256':digest.hexdigest(),'bytes':path.stat().st_size}))
    write('\nASSEMBLY SCRIPT (mechanical report reproducibility)')
    literal(scratch/'collect_measurements.py')
print(json.dumps(summary,indent=2))
